bfl_purchase_native_inventory = {
bfl_initialize_goods = yes
if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_good = 1 } set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_type value = goods:wheat } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_good = 2 } set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_type value = goods:salt } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_good = 3 } set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_type value = goods:iron } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_good = 4 } set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_type value = goods:cloth } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_good = 5 } set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_type value = goods:pepper } }
save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
        if = { limit = { exists = var:bfl_goods_type } var:bfl_goods_type = { save_scope_as = bfl_goods_execution } }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_amount } }
        set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_amount value = 10 } }
        if = { limit = { bfl_can_originate_commodity_inventory = yes bfl_goods_capacity = yes
            bfl_goods_market_access = yes exists = capital exists = var:bfl_goods_type
            var:bfl_goods_amount > 0 NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_guard }
            "var:bfl_goods_market.stockpile_in_market(scope:bfl_goods_execution)" >= var:bfl_goods_amount
            bfl_free_cash >= { value = "var:bfl_goods_market.market_price(scope:bfl_goods_execution)" multiply = var:bfl_goods_amount add = 1 } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_allocated value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cash_before value = gold }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_stock_before value = "var:bfl_goods_market.stockpile_in_market(scope:bfl_goods_execution)" }
            buy_goods_from_market = { market = var:bfl_goods_market goods = var:bfl_goods_type amount = var:bfl_goods_amount }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_acquired value = { value = var:bfl_goods_stock_before subtract = "var:bfl_goods_market.stockpile_in_market(scope:bfl_goods_execution)" } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cost value = { value = var:bfl_goods_cash_before subtract = gold } }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_acquired > 0 }
    if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_allocated = 0 var:bfl_inv_01_active = 0 }

bfl_process_goods = {
bfl_initialize_goods = yes save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
        if = { limit = { bfl_period > var:bfl_goods_branch_period }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_branch_access = yes bfl_free_cash >= 1 }
                add_gold = -1 var:bfl_goods_host = { add_gold = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = 1 }
            } else_if = { limit = { exists = var:bfl_goods_host }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_branch_due value = 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_branch_01_state value = 2 }
            }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_branch_period value = bfl_period }
        }
    if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_active = 1 bfl_period > var:bfl_inv_01_last_period }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_last_period value = bfl_period }
            # Spoilage is a physical loss, never new cash. Accepted forwards
            # retain their fixed delivery obligation for collateral recovery.
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_old_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_01_quantity }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_quantity multiply = 0.99833 }
            if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_01_state = 0 AND = { var:bfl_inv_01_state = 4 var:bfl_inv_01_state_before_seizure = 0 } } var:bfl_inv_01_basis > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_decay value = { value = var:bfl_inv_01_basis multiply = 0.00167 } }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_basis subtract = var:bfl_goods_decay }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = var:bfl_goods_decay }
            }
            if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_01_state = 1 var:bfl_inv_01_state = 2
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_01_state = 4 var:bfl_inv_01_state_before_seizure > 0 var:bfl_inv_01_state_before_seizure < 3 } } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_01_quantity } }
            if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_01_state = 1 AND = { var:bfl_inv_01_state = 4 var:bfl_inv_01_state_before_seizure = 1 } } var:bfl_inv_01_holder_basis > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_decay value = { value = var:bfl_inv_01_holder_basis multiply = 0.00167 } }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_holder_basis subtract = var:bfl_goods_decay }
                if = { limit = { country_exists = var:bfl_inv_01_holder var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                    var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_decay } } }
            }
            # Warehouse operating cost is 0.1 gold per lot per month. For
            # receipt holders it is a payable, never an unfunded forced debit.
            if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_01_state = 1 AND = { var:bfl_inv_01_state = 4 var:bfl_inv_01_state_before_seizure = 1 } } }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_storage_due add = 0.1 }
                if = { limit = { country_exists = var:bfl_inv_01_holder var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { gold >= prev.var:bfl_inv_01_storage_due
                    OR = { NOT = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } bfl_free_cash >= prev.var:bfl_inv_01_storage_due } } }
                    var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_storage_due multiply = -1 } }
                    if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                        var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_storage_due } } }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_storage_due value = 0 }
                }
            } else_if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_01_state = 0 var:bfl_inv_01_state = 2 AND = { var:bfl_inv_01_state = 4 OR = { var:bfl_inv_01_state_before_seizure = 0 var:bfl_inv_01_state_before_seizure = 2 } } } }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_expense_arrears add = 0.1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = 0.1 }
            }
            bfl_settle_goods_forward_01 = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_transit_due > 0 } bfl_redeem_goods_01 = yes }
        }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_active = 1 bfl_period > var:bfl_inv_02_last_period }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_last_period value = bfl_period }
            # Spoilage is a physical loss, never new cash. Accepted forwards
            # retain their fixed delivery obligation for collateral recovery.
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_old_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_02_quantity }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_quantity multiply = 0.99833 }
            if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_02_state = 0 AND = { var:bfl_inv_02_state = 4 var:bfl_inv_02_state_before_seizure = 0 } } var:bfl_inv_02_basis > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_decay value = { value = var:bfl_inv_02_basis multiply = 0.00167 } }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_basis subtract = var:bfl_goods_decay }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = var:bfl_goods_decay }
            }
            if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_02_state = 1 var:bfl_inv_02_state = 2
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_02_state = 4 var:bfl_inv_02_state_before_seizure > 0 var:bfl_inv_02_state_before_seizure < 3 } } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_02_quantity } }
            if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_02_state = 1 AND = { var:bfl_inv_02_state = 4 var:bfl_inv_02_state_before_seizure = 1 } } var:bfl_inv_02_holder_basis > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_decay value = { value = var:bfl_inv_02_holder_basis multiply = 0.00167 } }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_holder_basis subtract = var:bfl_goods_decay }
                if = { limit = { country_exists = var:bfl_inv_02_holder var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                    var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_decay } } }
            }
            # Warehouse operating cost is 0.1 gold per lot per month. For
            # receipt holders it is a payable, never an unfunded forced debit.
            if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_02_state = 1 AND = { var:bfl_inv_02_state = 4 var:bfl_inv_02_state_before_seizure = 1 } } }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_storage_due add = 0.1 }
                if = { limit = { country_exists = var:bfl_inv_02_holder var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { gold >= prev.var:bfl_inv_02_storage_due
                    OR = { NOT = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } bfl_free_cash >= prev.var:bfl_inv_02_storage_due } } }
                    var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_storage_due multiply = -1 } }
                    if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                        var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_storage_due } } }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_storage_due value = 0 }
                }
            } else_if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_02_state = 0 var:bfl_inv_02_state = 2 AND = { var:bfl_inv_02_state = 4 OR = { var:bfl_inv_02_state_before_seizure = 0 var:bfl_inv_02_state_before_seizure = 2 } } } }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_expense_arrears add = 0.1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = 0.1 }
            }
            bfl_settle_goods_forward_02 = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_transit_due > 0 } bfl_redeem_goods_02 = yes }
        }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_03_active = 1 bfl_period > var:bfl_inv_03_last_period }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_last_period value = bfl_period }
            # Spoilage is a physical loss, never new cash. Accepted forwards
            # retain their fixed delivery obligation for collateral recovery.
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_old_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_03_quantity }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_quantity multiply = 0.99833 }
            if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_03_state = 0 AND = { var:bfl_inv_03_state = 4 var:bfl_inv_03_state_before_seizure = 0 } } var:bfl_inv_03_basis > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_decay value = { value = var:bfl_inv_03_basis multiply = 0.00167 } }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_basis subtract = var:bfl_goods_decay }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = var:bfl_goods_decay }
            }
            if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_03_state = 1 var:bfl_inv_03_state = 2
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_03_state = 4 var:bfl_inv_03_state_before_seizure > 0 var:bfl_inv_03_state_before_seizure < 3 } } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_03_quantity } }
            if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_03_state = 1 AND = { var:bfl_inv_03_state = 4 var:bfl_inv_03_state_before_seizure = 1 } } var:bfl_inv_03_holder_basis > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_decay value = { value = var:bfl_inv_03_holder_basis multiply = 0.00167 } }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_holder_basis subtract = var:bfl_goods_decay }
                if = { limit = { country_exists = var:bfl_inv_03_holder var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                    var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_decay } } }
            }
            # Warehouse operating cost is 0.1 gold per lot per month. For
            # receipt holders it is a payable, never an unfunded forced debit.
            if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_03_state = 1 AND = { var:bfl_inv_03_state = 4 var:bfl_inv_03_state_before_seizure = 1 } } }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_storage_due add = 0.1 }
                if = { limit = { country_exists = var:bfl_inv_03_holder var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { gold >= prev.var:bfl_inv_03_storage_due
                    OR = { NOT = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } bfl_free_cash >= prev.var:bfl_inv_03_storage_due } } }
                    var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_storage_due multiply = -1 } }
                    if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                        var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_storage_due } } }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_storage_due value = 0 }
                }
            } else_if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_03_state = 0 var:bfl_inv_03_state = 2 AND = { var:bfl_inv_03_state = 4 OR = { var:bfl_inv_03_state_before_seizure = 0 var:bfl_inv_03_state_before_seizure = 2 } } } }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_expense_arrears add = 0.1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = 0.1 }
            }
            bfl_settle_goods_forward_03 = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_03_transit_due > 0 } bfl_redeem_goods_03 = yes }
        }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_04_active = 1 bfl_period > var:bfl_inv_04_last_period }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_last_period value = bfl_period }
            # Spoilage is a physical loss, never new cash. Accepted forwards

                    var:bfl_inv_16_holder = { change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_decay } } }
            }
            # Warehouse operating cost is 0.1 gold per lot per month. For
            # receipt holders it is a payable, never an unfunded forced debit.
            if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_16_state = 1 AND = { var:bfl_inv_16_state = 4 var:bfl_inv_16_state_before_seizure = 1 } } }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_storage_due add = 0.1 }
                if = { limit = { country_exists = var:bfl_inv_16_holder var:bfl_inv_16_holder = { gold >= prev.var:bfl_inv_16_storage_due
                    OR = { NOT = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } bfl_free_cash >= prev.var:bfl_inv_16_storage_due } } }
                    var:bfl_inv_16_holder = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_storage_due multiply = -1 } }
                    if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                        var:bfl_inv_16_holder = { change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_storage_due } } }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_storage_due value = 0 }
                }
            } else_if = { limit = { OR = { var:bfl_inv_16_state = 0 var:bfl_inv_16_state = 2 AND = { var:bfl_inv_16_state = 4 OR = { var:bfl_inv_16_state_before_seizure = 0 var:bfl_inv_16_state_before_seizure = 2 } } } }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_expense_arrears add = 0.1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward add = 0.1 }
            }
            bfl_settle_goods_forward_16 = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_transit_due > 0 } bfl_redeem_goods_16 = yes }
        }
bfl_refresh_goods_marks = yes
bfl_margin_goods_01 = yes
bfl_margin_goods_02 = yes
bfl_margin_goods_03 = yes
bfl_margin_goods_04 = yes
bfl_margin_goods_05 = yes
bfl_margin_goods_06 = yes
bfl_margin_goods_07 = yes
bfl_margin_goods_08 = yes
bfl_margin_goods_09 = yes
bfl_margin_goods_10 = yes
bfl_margin_goods_11 = yes
bfl_margin_goods_12 = yes
bfl_margin_goods_13 = yes
bfl_margin_goods_14 = yes
bfl_margin_goods_15 = yes
bfl_margin_goods_16 = yes
bfl_value_goods = yes bfl_manage_goods_policy = yes
}
bfl_book_goods_gain = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_realized >= 0 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_goods add = var:bfl_goods_realized } }
                else = { change_variable = { name = bfl_loss_carryforward subtract = var:bfl_goods_realized } }
}
bfl_sell_goods_01 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_sellable_01 = yes }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cash_before value = gold }
        sell_goods_to_market = { market = var:bfl_inv_01_market goods = var:bfl_inv_01_type amount = var:bfl_inv_01_quantity }
set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_cash_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_01_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_basis value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_offer value = 0 }
            remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
        }
}
bfl_list_goods_1_01 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_01 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_offer value = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_01_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_list_goods_2_01 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_01 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_offer value = 2 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_01_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_quote_goods_01 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_01 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_01_offer > 0 bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_01_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            scope:bfl_goods_party = {
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_bank value = scope:bfl_goods_bank }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_slot value = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_offer_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_price value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_quantity value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_mode value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_good value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_good }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_type value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_type }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_expiry value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_offer_expiry }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_quote_mode = 1 } trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.2 } else = { trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.6 }
            }
        }
}
bfl_accept_goods_01 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_offer > 0 bfl_goods_offerable_01 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_01_offer_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_generation
            var:bfl_inv_01_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation
            var:bfl_inv_01_price = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_price
            var:bfl_inv_01_quantity = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_quantity
            var:bfl_inv_01_offer = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_mode
            bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_01_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_01_offer = 1 bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes }
                    AND = { var:bfl_inv_01_offer = 2 bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes } }
            bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes
            NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_guard }
        }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = { value = var:bfl_inv_01_price add = 1 } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = var:bfl_inv_01_offer }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes }
                OR = { var:bfl_inv_01_offer = 1 bfl_free_cash >= { value = var:bfl_inv_01_price multiply = 1.25 } } }
                set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_01_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_01_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_state value = var:bfl_inv_01_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_accepted_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_01_quantity }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_quote multiply = -1 } }
                add_gold = bfl_goods_quote
                change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_state = 1 }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_01_price subtract = var:bfl_inv_01_basis } }
                    bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_basis value = 0 }
                } else = {
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_escrow value = var:bfl_inv_01_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_margin value = var:bfl_inv_01_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 3 } }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables add = var:bfl_inv_01_escrow }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = { value = var:bfl_inv_01_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_01_margin } }
                }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_offer value = 0 }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { remove_variable = bfl_goods_quote_bank }
                remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
                if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            }
        }
}
bfl_deliver_goods_01 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_01_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_01_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            bfl_goods_access_01 = yes var:bfl_inv_01_storage_due = 0
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { gold >= 1 exists = capital } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_market = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                bfl_redeem_goods_01 = yes
            } else = {
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = -1 }
                if = { limit = { exists = var:bfl_inv_01_host country_exists = var:bfl_inv_01_host } var:bfl_inv_01_host = { add_gold = 1 } }
                else = { add_gold = 1 change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_destination value = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_transit_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                # The accepted custody terms assign physical transport loss to the holder.
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_quantity multiply = 0.99 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_01_quantity }
            }
        }
}
bfl_redeem_goods_01 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_01_state = 1
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_01_holder exists = var:bfl_inv_01_holder.capital
            var:bfl_inv_01_storage_due = 0
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_01_transit_due = 0 var:bfl_inv_01_market = var:bfl_inv_01_holder.capital.market bfl_goods_access_01 = yes }
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_01_transit_due > 0 bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_01_transit_due var:bfl_inv_01_destination = var:bfl_inv_01_holder.capital.market } } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
        set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_before value = var:bfl_inv_01_holder.gold }
var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { sell_goods_to_market = { market = capital.market goods = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_type amount = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_quantity } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = var:bfl_inv_01_holder.gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_holder_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_01_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_state value = 3 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_encumbered value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_transit_due value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_transfer_goods_01 = {
save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = var:bfl_inv_01_price }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_01_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_01_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_seller country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_seller
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { scope:bfl_goods_party = scope:bfl_goods_seller }
            var:bfl_inv_01_transit_due = 0 bfl_goods_access_01 = yes
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes } }
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_price multiply = -1 } }
            scope:bfl_goods_seller = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                scope:bfl_goods_seller = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_price subtract = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_01_holder_basis } } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_01_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_settle_goods_forward_01 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_01_state = 2
            bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_01_due bfl_goods_access_01 = yes
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_01_holder gold >= { value = var:bfl_inv_01_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_01_margin } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_shortfall value = { value = 1 subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_01_quantity divide = var:bfl_inv_01_accepted_quantity } multiply = var:bfl_inv_01_price min = 0 max = var:bfl_inv_01_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_refund value = { value = var:bfl_goods_shortfall multiply = 2 } }
            add_gold = { value = bfl_goods_refund multiply = -1 }
            var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.bfl_goods_refund }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_refund > var:bfl_inv_01_holder_basis var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_01_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_holder_basis subtract = bfl_goods_refund }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_holder_basis value = { value = var:bfl_inv_01_holder_basis min = 0 } }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables subtract = var:bfl_inv_01_escrow }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_01_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_01_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_01_price subtract = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_01_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_basis value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_escrow value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_margin value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_state value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_quantity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_state value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_encumbered value = 0 }
            }
            bfl_value_goods = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_01_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_enforce_goods_01 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_01_state < 4
            var:bfl_inv_01_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            NOT = { bfl_goods_branch_access = yes } var:bfl_inv_01_transit_due = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_state_before_seizure value = var:bfl_inv_01_state }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_state value = 4 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_offer value = 0 }
        }
}
bfl_release_goods_01 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_01_state = 4
            var:bfl_inv_01_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party bfl_goods_branch_access = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_state value = var:bfl_inv_01_state_before_seizure }
        }
}
bfl_margin_goods_01 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_01_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_01_state = 2
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_required_margin value = { value = bfl_goods_spot_01 min = var:bfl_inv_01_price } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_margin_call value = { value = var:bfl_goods_required_margin subtract = var:bfl_inv_01_margin } }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 bfl_free_cash >= bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_margin add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
            } else_if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_01_due value = bfl_period }
                bfl_settle_goods_forward_01 = yes
            }
        }
}
bfl_sell_goods_02 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_sellable_02 = yes }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cash_before value = gold }
        sell_goods_to_market = { market = var:bfl_inv_02_market goods = var:bfl_inv_02_type amount = var:bfl_inv_02_quantity }
set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_cash_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_02_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_basis value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_offer value = 0 }
            remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
        }
}

                bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_offer value = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_02_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_list_goods_2_02 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_02 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_offer value = 2 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_02_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_quote_goods_02 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_02 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_02_offer > 0 bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_02_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            scope:bfl_goods_party = {
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_bank value = scope:bfl_goods_bank }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_slot value = 2 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_offer_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_price value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_quantity value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_mode value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_good value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_good }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_type value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_type }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_expiry value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_offer_expiry }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_quote_mode = 1 } trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.2 } else = { trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.6 }
            }
        }
}
bfl_accept_goods_02 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_offer > 0 bfl_goods_offerable_02 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_02_offer_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_generation
            var:bfl_inv_02_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation
            var:bfl_inv_02_price = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_price
            var:bfl_inv_02_quantity = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_quantity
            var:bfl_inv_02_offer = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_mode
            bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_02_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_02_offer = 1 bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes }
                    AND = { var:bfl_inv_02_offer = 2 bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes } }
            bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes
            NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_guard }
        }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = { value = var:bfl_inv_02_price add = 1 } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = var:bfl_inv_02_offer }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes }
                OR = { var:bfl_inv_02_offer = 1 bfl_free_cash >= { value = var:bfl_inv_02_price multiply = 1.25 } } }
                set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_02_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_02_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_state value = var:bfl_inv_02_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_accepted_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_02_quantity }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_quote multiply = -1 } }
                add_gold = bfl_goods_quote
                change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_state = 1 }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_02_price subtract = var:bfl_inv_02_basis } }
                    bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_basis value = 0 }
                } else = {
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_escrow value = var:bfl_inv_02_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_margin value = var:bfl_inv_02_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 3 } }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables add = var:bfl_inv_02_escrow }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = { value = var:bfl_inv_02_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_02_margin } }
                }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_offer value = 0 }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { remove_variable = bfl_goods_quote_bank }
                remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
                if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            }
        }
}
bfl_deliver_goods_02 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_02_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_02_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            bfl_goods_access_02 = yes var:bfl_inv_02_storage_due = 0
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { gold >= 1 exists = capital } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_market = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                bfl_redeem_goods_02 = yes
            } else = {
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = -1 }
                if = { limit = { exists = var:bfl_inv_02_host country_exists = var:bfl_inv_02_host } var:bfl_inv_02_host = { add_gold = 1 } }
                else = { add_gold = 1 change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_destination value = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_transit_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                # The accepted custody terms assign physical transport loss to the holder.
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_quantity multiply = 0.99 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_02_quantity }
            }
        }
}
bfl_redeem_goods_02 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_02_state = 1
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_02_holder exists = var:bfl_inv_02_holder.capital
            var:bfl_inv_02_storage_due = 0
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_02_transit_due = 0 var:bfl_inv_02_market = var:bfl_inv_02_holder.capital.market bfl_goods_access_02 = yes }
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_02_transit_due > 0 bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_02_transit_due var:bfl_inv_02_destination = var:bfl_inv_02_holder.capital.market } } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
        set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_before value = var:bfl_inv_02_holder.gold }
var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { sell_goods_to_market = { market = capital.market goods = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_type amount = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_quantity } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = var:bfl_inv_02_holder.gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_holder_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_02_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_state value = 3 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_encumbered value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_transit_due value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_transfer_goods_02 = {
save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = var:bfl_inv_02_price }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_02_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_02_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_seller country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_seller
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { scope:bfl_goods_party = scope:bfl_goods_seller }
            var:bfl_inv_02_transit_due = 0 bfl_goods_access_02 = yes
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes } }
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_price multiply = -1 } }
            scope:bfl_goods_seller = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                scope:bfl_goods_seller = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_price subtract = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_02_holder_basis } } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_02_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_settle_goods_forward_02 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_02_state = 2
            bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_02_due bfl_goods_access_02 = yes
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_02_holder gold >= { value = var:bfl_inv_02_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_02_margin } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_shortfall value = { value = 1 subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_02_quantity divide = var:bfl_inv_02_accepted_quantity } multiply = var:bfl_inv_02_price min = 0 max = var:bfl_inv_02_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_refund value = { value = var:bfl_goods_shortfall multiply = 2 } }
            add_gold = { value = bfl_goods_refund multiply = -1 }
            var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.bfl_goods_refund }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_refund > var:bfl_inv_02_holder_basis var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_02_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_holder_basis subtract = bfl_goods_refund }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_holder_basis value = { value = var:bfl_inv_02_holder_basis min = 0 } }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables subtract = var:bfl_inv_02_escrow }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_02_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_02_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_02_price subtract = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_02_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_basis value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_escrow value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_margin value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_state value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_quantity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_state value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_encumbered value = 0 }
            }
            bfl_value_goods = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_02_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_enforce_goods_02 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_02_state < 4
            var:bfl_inv_02_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            NOT = { bfl_goods_branch_access = yes } var:bfl_inv_02_transit_due = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_state_before_seizure value = var:bfl_inv_02_state }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_state value = 4 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_offer value = 0 }
        }
}
bfl_release_goods_02 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_02_state = 4
            var:bfl_inv_02_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party bfl_goods_branch_access = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_state value = var:bfl_inv_02_state_before_seizure }
        }
}
bfl_margin_goods_02 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_02_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_02_state = 2
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_required_margin value = { value = bfl_goods_spot_02 min = var:bfl_inv_02_price } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_margin_call value = { value = var:bfl_goods_required_margin subtract = var:bfl_inv_02_margin } }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 bfl_free_cash >= bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_margin add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
            } else_if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_02_due value = bfl_period }
                bfl_settle_goods_forward_02 = yes
            }
        }
}
bfl_sell_goods_03 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_sellable_03 = yes }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cash_before value = gold }
        sell_goods_to_market = { market = var:bfl_inv_03_market goods = var:bfl_inv_03_type amount = var:bfl_inv_03_quantity }
set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_cash_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_03_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_basis value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_offer value = 0 }
            remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
        }
}
bfl_list_goods_1_03 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_03 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_offer value = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_03_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_list_goods_2_03 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_03 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_offer value = 2 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_03_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_quote_goods_03 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_03 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_03_offer > 0 bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_03_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            scope:bfl_goods_party = {
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_bank value = scope:bfl_goods_bank }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_slot value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_offer_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_price value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_quantity value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_mode value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_good value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_good }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_type value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_type }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_expiry value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_offer_expiry }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_quote_mode = 1 } trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.2 } else = { trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.6 }
            }
        }
}
