bfl_redeem_goods_03 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_03_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_03_state = 1
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_03_holder exists = var:bfl_inv_03_holder.capital
            var:bfl_inv_03_storage_due = 0
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_03_transit_due = 0 var:bfl_inv_03_market = var:bfl_inv_03_holder.capital.market bfl_goods_access_03 = yes }
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_03_transit_due > 0 bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_03_transit_due var:bfl_inv_03_destination = var:bfl_inv_03_holder.capital.market } } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
        set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_before value = var:bfl_inv_03_holder.gold }
var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { sell_goods_to_market = { market = capital.market goods = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_type amount = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_quantity } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = var:bfl_inv_03_holder.gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_holder_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_03_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_state value = 3 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_encumbered value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_transit_due value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_transfer_goods_03 = {
save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = var:bfl_inv_03_price }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_03_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_03_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_03_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_seller country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_seller
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { scope:bfl_goods_party = scope:bfl_goods_seller }
            var:bfl_inv_03_transit_due = 0 bfl_goods_access_03 = yes
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes } }
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_price multiply = -1 } }
            scope:bfl_goods_seller = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                scope:bfl_goods_seller = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_price subtract = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_03_holder_basis } } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_03_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_settle_goods_forward_03 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_03_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_03_state = 2
            bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_03_due bfl_goods_access_03 = yes
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_03_holder gold >= { value = var:bfl_inv_03_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_03_margin } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_shortfall value = { value = 1 subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_03_quantity divide = var:bfl_inv_03_accepted_quantity } multiply = var:bfl_inv_03_price min = 0 max = var:bfl_inv_03_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_refund value = { value = var:bfl_goods_shortfall multiply = 2 } }
            add_gold = { value = bfl_goods_refund multiply = -1 }
            var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.bfl_goods_refund }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_refund > var:bfl_inv_03_holder_basis var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_03_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_holder_basis subtract = bfl_goods_refund }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_holder_basis value = { value = var:bfl_inv_03_holder_basis min = 0 } }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables subtract = var:bfl_inv_03_escrow }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_03_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_03_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_03_price subtract = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_03_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_basis value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_escrow value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_margin value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_state value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_03_quantity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_state value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_encumbered value = 0 }
            }
            bfl_value_goods = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_03_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_enforce_goods_03 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_03_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_03_state < 4
            var:bfl_inv_03_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            NOT = { bfl_goods_branch_access = yes } var:bfl_inv_03_transit_due = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_state_before_seizure value = var:bfl_inv_03_state }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_state value = 4 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_offer value = 0 }
        }
}
bfl_release_goods_03 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_03_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_03_state = 4
            var:bfl_inv_03_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party bfl_goods_branch_access = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_state value = var:bfl_inv_03_state_before_seizure }
        }
}
bfl_margin_goods_03 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_03_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_03_state = 2
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_required_margin value = { value = bfl_goods_spot_03 min = var:bfl_inv_03_price } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_margin_call value = { value = var:bfl_goods_required_margin subtract = var:bfl_inv_03_margin } }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 bfl_free_cash >= bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_margin add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
            } else_if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_03_due value = bfl_period }
                bfl_settle_goods_forward_03 = yes
            }
        }
}
bfl_sell_goods_04 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_sellable_04 = yes }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cash_before value = gold }
        sell_goods_to_market = { market = var:bfl_inv_04_market goods = var:bfl_inv_04_type amount = var:bfl_inv_04_quantity }
set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_cash_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_04_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_basis value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_offer value = 0 }
            remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
        }
}
bfl_list_goods_1_04 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_04 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_offer value = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_04_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_list_goods_2_04 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_04 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_offer value = 2 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_04_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_quote_goods_04 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_04 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_04_offer > 0 bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_04_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            scope:bfl_goods_party = {
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_bank value = scope:bfl_goods_bank }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_slot value = 4 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_04_offer_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_04_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_price value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_04_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_quantity value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_04_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_mode value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_04_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_good value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_04_good }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_type value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_04_type }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_expiry value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_04_offer_expiry }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_quote_mode = 1 } trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.2 } else = { trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.6 }
            }
        }
}
bfl_accept_goods_04 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_04_offer > 0 bfl_goods_offerable_04 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_04_offer_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_generation
            var:bfl_inv_04_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation
            var:bfl_inv_04_price = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_price
            var:bfl_inv_04_quantity = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_quantity
            var:bfl_inv_04_offer = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_mode
            bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_04_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_04_offer = 1 bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes }
                    AND = { var:bfl_inv_04_offer = 2 bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes } }
            bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes
            NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_guard }
        }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = { value = var:bfl_inv_04_price add = 1 } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = var:bfl_inv_04_offer }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes }
                OR = { var:bfl_inv_04_offer = 1 bfl_free_cash >= { value = var:bfl_inv_04_price multiply = 1.25 } } }
                set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_04_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_04_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_state value = var:bfl_inv_04_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_accepted_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_04_quantity }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_quote multiply = -1 } }
                add_gold = bfl_goods_quote
                change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_04_state = 1 }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_04_price subtract = var:bfl_inv_04_basis } }
                    bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_basis value = 0 }
                } else = {
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_escrow value = var:bfl_inv_04_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_margin value = var:bfl_inv_04_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 3 } }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables add = var:bfl_inv_04_escrow }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = { value = var:bfl_inv_04_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_04_margin } }
                }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_offer value = 0 }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { remove_variable = bfl_goods_quote_bank }
                remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
                if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            }
        }
}
bfl_deliver_goods_04 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_04_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_04_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_04_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            bfl_goods_access_04 = yes var:bfl_inv_04_storage_due = 0
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { gold >= 1 exists = capital } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_04_market = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                bfl_redeem_goods_04 = yes
            } else = {
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = -1 }
                if = { limit = { exists = var:bfl_inv_04_host country_exists = var:bfl_inv_04_host } var:bfl_inv_04_host = { add_gold = 1 } }
                else = { add_gold = 1 change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_destination value = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_transit_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                # The accepted custody terms assign physical transport loss to the holder.
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_quantity multiply = 0.99 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_04_quantity }
            }
        }
}
bfl_redeem_goods_04 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_04_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_04_state = 1
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_04_holder exists = var:bfl_inv_04_holder.capital
            var:bfl_inv_04_storage_due = 0
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_04_transit_due = 0 var:bfl_inv_04_market = var:bfl_inv_04_holder.capital.market bfl_goods_access_04 = yes }
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_04_transit_due > 0 bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_04_transit_due var:bfl_inv_04_destination = var:bfl_inv_04_holder.capital.market } } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
        set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_before value = var:bfl_inv_04_holder.gold }
var:bfl_inv_04_holder = { sell_goods_to_market = { market = capital.market goods = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_04_type amount = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_04_quantity } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_04_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = var:bfl_inv_04_holder.gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_holder_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_04_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_04_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_state value = 3 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_encumbered value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_transit_due value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_04_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_04_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_transfer_goods_04 = {
save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = var:bfl_inv_04_price }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_04_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_04_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_04_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_seller country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_seller
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { scope:bfl_goods_party = scope:bfl_goods_seller }
            var:bfl_inv_04_transit_due = 0 bfl_goods_access_04 = yes
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes } }
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_04_price multiply = -1 } }
            scope:bfl_goods_seller = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_04_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                scope:bfl_goods_seller = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_04_price subtract = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_04_holder_basis } } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_04_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_settle_goods_forward_04 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_04_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_04_state = 2
            bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_04_due bfl_goods_access_04 = yes
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_04_holder gold >= { value = var:bfl_inv_04_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_04_margin } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_shortfall value = { value = 1 subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_04_quantity divide = var:bfl_inv_04_accepted_quantity } multiply = var:bfl_inv_04_price min = 0 max = var:bfl_inv_04_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_refund value = { value = var:bfl_goods_shortfall multiply = 2 } }
            add_gold = { value = bfl_goods_refund multiply = -1 }
            var:bfl_inv_04_holder = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.bfl_goods_refund }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_refund > var:bfl_inv_04_holder_basis var:bfl_inv_04_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_04_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_04_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_holder_basis subtract = bfl_goods_refund }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_holder_basis value = { value = var:bfl_inv_04_holder_basis min = 0 } }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables subtract = var:bfl_inv_04_escrow }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_04_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_04_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_04_price subtract = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_04_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_basis value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_escrow value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_margin value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_state value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_04_quantity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_state value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_encumbered value = 0 }
            }
            bfl_value_goods = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_04_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_04_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_enforce_goods_04 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_04_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_04_state < 4
            var:bfl_inv_04_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            NOT = { bfl_goods_branch_access = yes } var:bfl_inv_04_transit_due = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_state_before_seizure value = var:bfl_inv_04_state }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_state value = 4 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_offer value = 0 }
        }
}
bfl_release_goods_04 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_04_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_04_state = 4
            var:bfl_inv_04_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party bfl_goods_branch_access = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_state value = var:bfl_inv_04_state_before_seizure }
        }
}
bfl_margin_goods_04 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_04_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_04_state = 2
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_required_margin value = { value = bfl_goods_spot_04 min = var:bfl_inv_04_price } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_margin_call value = { value = var:bfl_goods_required_margin subtract = var:bfl_inv_04_margin } }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 bfl_free_cash >= bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_margin add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
            } else_if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_04_due value = bfl_period }
                bfl_settle_goods_forward_04 = yes
            }
        }
}
bfl_sell_goods_05 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_sellable_05 = yes }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cash_before value = gold }
        sell_goods_to_market = { market = var:bfl_inv_05_market goods = var:bfl_inv_05_type amount = var:bfl_inv_05_quantity }
set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_cash_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_05_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_basis value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_offer value = 0 }
            remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
        }
}
bfl_list_goods_1_05 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_05 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_offer value = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_05_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_list_goods_2_05 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_05 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_offer value = 2 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_05_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_quote_goods_05 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_05 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_05_offer > 0 bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_05_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            scope:bfl_goods_party = {
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_bank value = scope:bfl_goods_bank }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_slot value = 5 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_05_offer_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_05_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_price value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_05_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_quantity value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_05_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_mode value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_05_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_good value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_05_good }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_type value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_05_type }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_expiry value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_05_offer_expiry }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_quote_mode = 1 } trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.2 } else = { trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.6 }
            }
        }
}
bfl_accept_goods_05 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_05_offer > 0 bfl_goods_offerable_05 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_05_offer_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_generation
            var:bfl_inv_05_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation
            var:bfl_inv_05_price = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_price
            var:bfl_inv_05_quantity = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_quantity
            var:bfl_inv_05_offer = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_mode
            bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_05_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_05_offer = 1 bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes }
                    AND = { var:bfl_inv_05_offer = 2 bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes } }
            bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes
            NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_guard }
        }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = { value = var:bfl_inv_05_price add = 1 } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = var:bfl_inv_05_offer }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes }
                OR = { var:bfl_inv_05_offer = 1 bfl_free_cash >= { value = var:bfl_inv_05_price multiply = 1.25 } } }
                set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_05_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_05_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_state value = var:bfl_inv_05_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_accepted_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_05_quantity }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_quote multiply = -1 } }
                add_gold = bfl_goods_quote
                change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_05_state = 1 }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_05_price subtract = var:bfl_inv_05_basis } }
                    bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_basis value = 0 }
                } else = {
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_escrow value = var:bfl_inv_05_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_margin value = var:bfl_inv_05_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 3 } }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables add = var:bfl_inv_05_escrow }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = { value = var:bfl_inv_05_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_05_margin } }
                }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_offer value = 0 }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { remove_variable = bfl_goods_quote_bank }
                remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
                if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            }
        }
}
bfl_deliver_goods_05 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_05_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_05_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_05_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            bfl_goods_access_05 = yes var:bfl_inv_05_storage_due = 0
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { gold >= 1 exists = capital } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_05_market = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                bfl_redeem_goods_05 = yes
            } else = {
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = -1 }
                if = { limit = { exists = var:bfl_inv_05_host country_exists = var:bfl_inv_05_host } var:bfl_inv_05_host = { add_gold = 1 } }
                else = { add_gold = 1 change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_destination value = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_transit_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                # The accepted custody terms assign physical transport loss to the holder.
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_quantity multiply = 0.99 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_05_quantity }
            }
        }
}
bfl_redeem_goods_05 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_05_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_05_state = 1
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_05_holder exists = var:bfl_inv_05_holder.capital
            var:bfl_inv_05_storage_due = 0
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_05_transit_due = 0 var:bfl_inv_05_market = var:bfl_inv_05_holder.capital.market bfl_goods_access_05 = yes }
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_05_transit_due > 0 bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_05_transit_due var:bfl_inv_05_destination = var:bfl_inv_05_holder.capital.market } } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
        set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_before value = var:bfl_inv_05_holder.gold }
var:bfl_inv_05_holder = { sell_goods_to_market = { market = capital.market goods = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_05_type amount = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_05_quantity } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_05_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = var:bfl_inv_05_holder.gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_holder_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_05_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_05_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_state value = 3 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_encumbered value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_transit_due value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_05_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_05_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_transfer_goods_05 = {
save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = var:bfl_inv_05_price }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_05_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_05_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_05_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_seller country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_seller
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { scope:bfl_goods_party = scope:bfl_goods_seller }
            var:bfl_inv_05_transit_due = 0 bfl_goods_access_05 = yes
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes } }
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_05_price multiply = -1 } }
            scope:bfl_goods_seller = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_05_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                scope:bfl_goods_seller = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_05_price subtract = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_05_holder_basis } } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_05_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_settle_goods_forward_05 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_05_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_05_state = 2
            bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_05_due bfl_goods_access_05 = yes
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_05_holder gold >= { value = var:bfl_inv_05_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_05_margin } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_shortfall value = { value = 1 subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_05_quantity divide = var:bfl_inv_05_accepted_quantity } multiply = var:bfl_inv_05_price min = 0 max = var:bfl_inv_05_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_refund value = { value = var:bfl_goods_shortfall multiply = 2 } }
            add_gold = { value = bfl_goods_refund multiply = -1 }
            var:bfl_inv_05_holder = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.bfl_goods_refund }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_refund > var:bfl_inv_05_holder_basis var:bfl_inv_05_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_05_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_05_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_holder_basis subtract = bfl_goods_refund }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_holder_basis value = { value = var:bfl_inv_05_holder_basis min = 0 } }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables subtract = var:bfl_inv_05_escrow }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_05_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_05_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_05_price subtract = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_05_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_basis value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_escrow value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_margin value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_state value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_05_quantity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_state value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_encumbered value = 0 }
            }
            bfl_value_goods = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_05_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_05_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_enforce_goods_05 = {

            NOT = { bfl_goods_branch_access = yes } var:bfl_inv_05_transit_due = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_05_state_before_seizure value = var:bfl_inv_05_state }

                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_good value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_06_good }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_type value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_06_type }

            }
        }
}
bfl_accept_goods_06 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_06_offer > 0 bfl_goods_offerable_06 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_06_offer_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_generation
            var:bfl_inv_06_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation
            var:bfl_inv_06_price = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_price
            var:bfl_inv_06_quantity = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_quantity
            var:bfl_inv_06_offer = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_mode
            bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_06_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_06_offer = 1 bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes }
                    AND = { var:bfl_inv_06_offer = 2 bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes } }
            bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes
            NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_guard }
        }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = { value = var:bfl_inv_06_price add = 1 } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = var:bfl_inv_06_offer }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes }
                OR = { var:bfl_inv_06_offer = 1 bfl_free_cash >= { value = var:bfl_inv_06_price multiply = 1.25 } } }
                set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_06_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_06_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_state value = var:bfl_inv_06_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_accepted_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_06_quantity }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_quote multiply = -1 } }
                add_gold = bfl_goods_quote
                change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_06_state = 1 }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_06_price subtract = var:bfl_inv_06_basis } }
                    bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_basis value = 0 }
                } else = {
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_escrow value = var:bfl_inv_06_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_margin value = var:bfl_inv_06_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 3 } }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables add = var:bfl_inv_06_escrow }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = { value = var:bfl_inv_06_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_06_margin } }
                }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_offer value = 0 }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { remove_variable = bfl_goods_quote_bank }
                remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
                if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            }
        }
}
bfl_deliver_goods_06 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_06_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_06_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_06_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            bfl_goods_access_06 = yes var:bfl_inv_06_storage_due = 0
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { gold >= 1 exists = capital } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_06_market = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                bfl_redeem_goods_06 = yes
            } else = {
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = -1 }
                if = { limit = { exists = var:bfl_inv_06_host country_exists = var:bfl_inv_06_host } var:bfl_inv_06_host = { add_gold = 1 } }
                else = { add_gold = 1 change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_destination value = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_transit_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                # The accepted custody terms assign physical transport loss to the holder.
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_quantity multiply = 0.99 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_06_quantity }
            }
        }
}
bfl_redeem_goods_06 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_06_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_06_state = 1
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_06_holder exists = var:bfl_inv_06_holder.capital
            var:bfl_inv_06_storage_due = 0
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_06_transit_due = 0 var:bfl_inv_06_market = var:bfl_inv_06_holder.capital.market bfl_goods_access_06 = yes }
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_06_transit_due > 0 bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_06_transit_due var:bfl_inv_06_destination = var:bfl_inv_06_holder.capital.market } } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
        set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_before value = var:bfl_inv_06_holder.gold }
var:bfl_inv_06_holder = { sell_goods_to_market = { market = capital.market goods = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_06_type amount = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_06_quantity } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_06_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = var:bfl_inv_06_holder.gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_holder_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_06_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_06_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_state value = 3 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_encumbered value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_transit_due value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_06_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_06_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_transfer_goods_06 = {
save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = var:bfl_inv_06_price }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_06_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_06_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_06_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_seller country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_seller
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { scope:bfl_goods_party = scope:bfl_goods_seller }
            var:bfl_inv_06_transit_due = 0 bfl_goods_access_06 = yes
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes } }
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_06_price multiply = -1 } }
            scope:bfl_goods_seller = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_06_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                scope:bfl_goods_seller = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_06_price subtract = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_06_holder_basis } } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_06_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_settle_goods_forward_06 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_06_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_06_state = 2
            bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_06_due bfl_goods_access_06 = yes
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_06_holder gold >= { value = var:bfl_inv_06_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_06_margin } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_shortfall value = { value = 1 subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_06_quantity divide = var:bfl_inv_06_accepted_quantity } multiply = var:bfl_inv_06_price min = 0 max = var:bfl_inv_06_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_refund value = { value = var:bfl_goods_shortfall multiply = 2 } }
            add_gold = { value = bfl_goods_refund multiply = -1 }
            var:bfl_inv_06_holder = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.bfl_goods_refund }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_refund > var:bfl_inv_06_holder_basis var:bfl_inv_06_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_06_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_06_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_holder_basis subtract = bfl_goods_refund }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_holder_basis value = { value = var:bfl_inv_06_holder_basis min = 0 } }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables subtract = var:bfl_inv_06_escrow }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_06_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_06_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_06_price subtract = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_06_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_basis value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_escrow value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_margin value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_state value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_06_quantity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_state value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_encumbered value = 0 }
            }
            bfl_value_goods = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_06_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_06_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_enforce_goods_06 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_06_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_06_state < 4
            var:bfl_inv_06_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            NOT = { bfl_goods_branch_access = yes } var:bfl_inv_06_transit_due = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_state_before_seizure value = var:bfl_inv_06_state }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_state value = 4 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_offer value = 0 }
        }
}
bfl_release_goods_06 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_06_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_06_state = 4
            var:bfl_inv_06_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party bfl_goods_branch_access = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_state value = var:bfl_inv_06_state_before_seizure }
        }
}
bfl_margin_goods_06 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_06_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_06_state = 2
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_required_margin value = { value = bfl_goods_spot_06 min = var:bfl_inv_06_price } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_margin_call value = { value = var:bfl_goods_required_margin subtract = var:bfl_inv_06_margin } }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 bfl_free_cash >= bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_margin add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
            } else_if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_06_due value = bfl_period }
                bfl_settle_goods_forward_06 = yes
            }
        }
}
bfl_sell_goods_07 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_sellable_07 = yes }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cash_before value = gold }
        sell_goods_to_market = { market = var:bfl_inv_07_market goods = var:bfl_inv_07_type amount = var:bfl_inv_07_quantity }
set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_cash_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_07_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_basis value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_offer value = 0 }
            remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
        }
}
bfl_list_goods_1_07 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_07 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_offer value = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_07_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_list_goods_2_07 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_07 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_offer value = 2 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_07_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_quote_goods_07 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_07 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_07_offer > 0 bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_07_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            scope:bfl_goods_party = {
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_bank value = scope:bfl_goods_bank }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_slot value = 7 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_07_offer_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_07_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_price value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_07_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_quantity value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_07_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_mode value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_07_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_good value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_07_good }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_type value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_07_type }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_expiry value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_07_offer_expiry }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_quote_mode = 1 } trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.2 } else = { trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.6 }
            }
        }
}

bfl_redeem_goods_07 = {

            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_07_holder exists = var:bfl_inv_07_holder.capital
            var:bfl_inv_07_storage_due = 0
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_07_transit_due = 0 var:bfl_inv_07_market = var:bfl_inv_07_holder.capital.market bfl_goods_access_07 = yes }
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_07_transit_due > 0 bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_07_transit_due var:bfl_inv_07_destination = var:bfl_inv_07_holder.capital.market } } }

        set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_before value = var:bfl_inv_07_holder.gold }

if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_07_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = var:bfl_inv_07_holder.gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_holder_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_07_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_07_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_state value = 3 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_encumbered value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_transit_due value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_07_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_07_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_transfer_goods_07 = {
save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = var:bfl_inv_07_price }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_07_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_07_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_07_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_seller country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_seller
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { scope:bfl_goods_party = scope:bfl_goods_seller }
            var:bfl_inv_07_transit_due = 0 bfl_goods_access_07 = yes
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes } }
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_07_price multiply = -1 } }
            scope:bfl_goods_seller = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_07_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                scope:bfl_goods_seller = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_07_price subtract = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_07_holder_basis } } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_07_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_settle_goods_forward_07 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_07_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_07_state = 2
            bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_07_due bfl_goods_access_07 = yes
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_07_holder gold >= { value = var:bfl_inv_07_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_07_margin } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_shortfall value = { value = 1 subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_07_quantity divide = var:bfl_inv_07_accepted_quantity } multiply = var:bfl_inv_07_price min = 0 max = var:bfl_inv_07_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_refund value = { value = var:bfl_goods_shortfall multiply = 2 } }
            add_gold = { value = bfl_goods_refund multiply = -1 }
            var:bfl_inv_07_holder = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.bfl_goods_refund }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_refund > var:bfl_inv_07_holder_basis var:bfl_inv_07_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_07_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_07_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_holder_basis subtract = bfl_goods_refund }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_holder_basis value = { value = var:bfl_inv_07_holder_basis min = 0 } }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables subtract = var:bfl_inv_07_escrow }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_07_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_07_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_07_price subtract = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_07_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_basis value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_escrow value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_margin value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_state value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_07_quantity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_state value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_encumbered value = 0 }
            }
            bfl_value_goods = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_07_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_07_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_enforce_goods_07 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_07_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_07_state < 4
            var:bfl_inv_07_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            NOT = { bfl_goods_branch_access = yes } var:bfl_inv_07_transit_due = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_state_before_seizure value = var:bfl_inv_07_state }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_state value = 4 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_offer value = 0 }
        }
}
bfl_release_goods_07 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_07_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_07_state = 4
            var:bfl_inv_07_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party bfl_goods_branch_access = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_state value = var:bfl_inv_07_state_before_seizure }
        }
}
bfl_margin_goods_07 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_07_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_07_state = 2
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_required_margin value = { value = bfl_goods_spot_07 min = var:bfl_inv_07_price } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_margin_call value = { value = var:bfl_goods_required_margin subtract = var:bfl_inv_07_margin } }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 bfl_free_cash >= bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_margin add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
            } else_if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_07_due value = bfl_period }
                bfl_settle_goods_forward_07 = yes
            }
        }
}
bfl_sell_goods_08 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_sellable_08 = yes }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cash_before value = gold }
        sell_goods_to_market = { market = var:bfl_inv_08_market goods = var:bfl_inv_08_type amount = var:bfl_inv_08_quantity }
set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_cash_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_08_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_basis value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_offer value = 0 }
            remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
        }
}
bfl_list_goods_1_08 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_08 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_offer value = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_08_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_list_goods_2_08 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_08 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_offer value = 2 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_08_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_quote_goods_08 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_08 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_08_offer > 0 bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_08_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            scope:bfl_goods_party = {
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_bank value = scope:bfl_goods_bank }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_slot value = 8 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_08_offer_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_08_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_price value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_08_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_quantity value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_08_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_mode value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_08_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_good value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_08_good }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_type value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_08_type }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_expiry value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_08_offer_expiry }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_quote_mode = 1 } trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.2 } else = { trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.6 }
            }
        }
}
bfl_accept_goods_08 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_08_offer > 0 bfl_goods_offerable_08 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_08_offer_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_generation
            var:bfl_inv_08_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation
            var:bfl_inv_08_price = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_price
            var:bfl_inv_08_quantity = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_quantity
            var:bfl_inv_08_offer = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_mode
            bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_08_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_08_offer = 1 bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes }
                    AND = { var:bfl_inv_08_offer = 2 bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes } }
            bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes
            NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_guard }
        }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = { value = var:bfl_inv_08_price add = 1 } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = var:bfl_inv_08_offer }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes }
                OR = { var:bfl_inv_08_offer = 1 bfl_free_cash >= { value = var:bfl_inv_08_price multiply = 1.25 } } }
                set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_08_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_08_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_state value = var:bfl_inv_08_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_accepted_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_08_quantity }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_quote multiply = -1 } }
                add_gold = bfl_goods_quote
                change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_08_state = 1 }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_08_price subtract = var:bfl_inv_08_basis } }
                    bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_basis value = 0 }
                } else = {
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_escrow value = var:bfl_inv_08_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_margin value = var:bfl_inv_08_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 3 } }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables add = var:bfl_inv_08_escrow }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = { value = var:bfl_inv_08_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_08_margin } }
                }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_offer value = 0 }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { remove_variable = bfl_goods_quote_bank }
                remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
                if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            }
        }
}
bfl_deliver_goods_08 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_08_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_08_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_08_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            bfl_goods_access_08 = yes var:bfl_inv_08_storage_due = 0
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { gold >= 1 exists = capital } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_08_market = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                bfl_redeem_goods_08 = yes
            } else = {
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = -1 }
                if = { limit = { exists = var:bfl_inv_08_host country_exists = var:bfl_inv_08_host } var:bfl_inv_08_host = { add_gold = 1 } }
                else = { add_gold = 1 change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_destination value = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_transit_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                # The accepted custody terms assign physical transport loss to the holder.
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_quantity multiply = 0.99 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_08_quantity }
            }
        }
}
bfl_redeem_goods_08 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_08_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_08_state = 1
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_08_holder exists = var:bfl_inv_08_holder.capital
            var:bfl_inv_08_storage_due = 0
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_08_transit_due = 0 var:bfl_inv_08_market = var:bfl_inv_08_holder.capital.market bfl_goods_access_08 = yes }
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_08_transit_due > 0 bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_08_transit_due var:bfl_inv_08_destination = var:bfl_inv_08_holder.capital.market } } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
        set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_before value = var:bfl_inv_08_holder.gold }
var:bfl_inv_08_holder = { sell_goods_to_market = { market = capital.market goods = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_08_type amount = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_08_quantity } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_08_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = var:bfl_inv_08_holder.gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_holder_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_08_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_08_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_state value = 3 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_encumbered value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_transit_due value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_08_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_08_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_transfer_goods_08 = {
save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = var:bfl_inv_08_price }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_08_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_08_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_08_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_seller country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_seller

            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}

bfl_release_goods_08 = {
