            var:bfl_inv_08_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party bfl_goods_branch_access = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_state value = var:bfl_inv_08_state_before_seizure }
        }
}
bfl_margin_goods_08 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_08_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_08_state = 2
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_required_margin value = { value = bfl_goods_spot_08 min = var:bfl_inv_08_price } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_margin_call value = { value = var:bfl_goods_required_margin subtract = var:bfl_inv_08_margin } }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 bfl_free_cash >= bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_margin add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
            } else_if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_08_due value = bfl_period }
                bfl_settle_goods_forward_08 = yes
            }
        }
}
bfl_sell_goods_09 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_sellable_09 = yes }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cash_before value = gold }
        sell_goods_to_market = { market = var:bfl_inv_09_market goods = var:bfl_inv_09_type amount = var:bfl_inv_09_quantity }
set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_cash_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_09_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_basis value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_offer value = 0 }
            remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
        }
}
bfl_list_goods_1_09 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_09 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_offer value = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_09_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_list_goods_2_09 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_09 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_offer value = 2 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_09_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_quote_goods_09 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_09 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_09_offer > 0 bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_09_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            scope:bfl_goods_party = {
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_bank value = scope:bfl_goods_bank }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_slot value = 9 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_09_offer_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_09_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_price value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_09_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_quantity value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_09_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_mode value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_09_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_good value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_09_good }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_type value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_09_type }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_expiry value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_09_offer_expiry }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_quote_mode = 1 } trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.2 } else = { trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.6 }
            }
        }
}
bfl_accept_goods_09 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_09_offer > 0 bfl_goods_offerable_09 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_09_offer_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_generation
            var:bfl_inv_09_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation
            var:bfl_inv_09_price = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_price
            var:bfl_inv_09_quantity = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_quantity
            var:bfl_inv_09_offer = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_mode
            bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_09_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_09_offer = 1 bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes }
                    AND = { var:bfl_inv_09_offer = 2 bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes } }
            bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes
            NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_guard }
        }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = { value = var:bfl_inv_09_price add = 1 } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = var:bfl_inv_09_offer }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes }
                OR = { var:bfl_inv_09_offer = 1 bfl_free_cash >= { value = var:bfl_inv_09_price multiply = 1.25 } } }
                set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_09_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_09_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_state value = var:bfl_inv_09_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_accepted_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_09_quantity }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_quote multiply = -1 } }
                add_gold = bfl_goods_quote
                change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_09_state = 1 }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_09_price subtract = var:bfl_inv_09_basis } }
                    bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_basis value = 0 }
                } else = {
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_escrow value = var:bfl_inv_09_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_margin value = var:bfl_inv_09_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 3 } }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables add = var:bfl_inv_09_escrow }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = { value = var:bfl_inv_09_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_09_margin } }
                }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_offer value = 0 }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { remove_variable = bfl_goods_quote_bank }
                remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
                if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            }
        }
}
bfl_deliver_goods_09 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_09_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_09_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_09_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            bfl_goods_access_09 = yes var:bfl_inv_09_storage_due = 0
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { gold >= 1 exists = capital } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_09_market = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                bfl_redeem_goods_09 = yes
            } else = {
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = -1 }
                if = { limit = { exists = var:bfl_inv_09_host country_exists = var:bfl_inv_09_host } var:bfl_inv_09_host = { add_gold = 1 } }
                else = { add_gold = 1 change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_destination value = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_transit_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                # The accepted custody terms assign physical transport loss to the holder.
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_quantity multiply = 0.99 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_09_quantity }
            }
        }
}
bfl_redeem_goods_09 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_09_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_09_state = 1
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_09_holder exists = var:bfl_inv_09_holder.capital
            var:bfl_inv_09_storage_due = 0
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_09_transit_due = 0 var:bfl_inv_09_market = var:bfl_inv_09_holder.capital.market bfl_goods_access_09 = yes }
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_09_transit_due > 0 bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_09_transit_due var:bfl_inv_09_destination = var:bfl_inv_09_holder.capital.market } } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
        set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_before value = var:bfl_inv_09_holder.gold }
var:bfl_inv_09_holder = { sell_goods_to_market = { market = capital.market goods = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_09_type amount = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_09_quantity } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_09_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = var:bfl_inv_09_holder.gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_holder_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_09_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_09_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_state value = 3 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_encumbered value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_transit_due value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_09_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_09_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_transfer_goods_09 = {
save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = var:bfl_inv_09_price }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_09_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_09_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_09_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_seller country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_seller
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { scope:bfl_goods_party = scope:bfl_goods_seller }
            var:bfl_inv_09_transit_due = 0 bfl_goods_access_09 = yes
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes } }
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_09_price multiply = -1 } }
            scope:bfl_goods_seller = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_09_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                scope:bfl_goods_seller = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_09_price subtract = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_09_holder_basis } } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_09_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_settle_goods_forward_09 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_09_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_09_state = 2
            bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_09_due bfl_goods_access_09 = yes
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_09_holder gold >= { value = var:bfl_inv_09_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_09_margin } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_shortfall value = { value = 1 subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_09_quantity divide = var:bfl_inv_09_accepted_quantity } multiply = var:bfl_inv_09_price min = 0 max = var:bfl_inv_09_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_refund value = { value = var:bfl_goods_shortfall multiply = 2 } }
            add_gold = { value = bfl_goods_refund multiply = -1 }
            var:bfl_inv_09_holder = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.bfl_goods_refund }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_refund > var:bfl_inv_09_holder_basis var:bfl_inv_09_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_09_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_09_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_holder_basis subtract = bfl_goods_refund }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_holder_basis value = { value = var:bfl_inv_09_holder_basis min = 0 } }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables subtract = var:bfl_inv_09_escrow }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_09_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_09_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_09_price subtract = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_09_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_basis value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_escrow value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_margin value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_state value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_09_quantity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_state value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_encumbered value = 0 }
            }
            bfl_value_goods = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_09_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_09_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_enforce_goods_09 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_09_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_09_state < 4
            var:bfl_inv_09_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            NOT = { bfl_goods_branch_access = yes } var:bfl_inv_09_transit_due = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_state_before_seizure value = var:bfl_inv_09_state }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_state value = 4 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_offer value = 0 }
        }
}
bfl_release_goods_09 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_09_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_09_state = 4
            var:bfl_inv_09_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party bfl_goods_branch_access = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_state value = var:bfl_inv_09_state_before_seizure }
        }
}
bfl_margin_goods_09 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_09_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_09_state = 2
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_required_margin value = { value = bfl_goods_spot_09 min = var:bfl_inv_09_price } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_margin_call value = { value = var:bfl_goods_required_margin subtract = var:bfl_inv_09_margin } }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 bfl_free_cash >= bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_margin add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
            } else_if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_09_due value = bfl_period }
                bfl_settle_goods_forward_09 = yes
            }
        }
}

bfl_accept_goods_10 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_10_offer > 0 bfl_goods_offerable_10 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_10_offer_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_generation
            var:bfl_inv_10_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation
            var:bfl_inv_10_price = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_price
            var:bfl_inv_10_quantity = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_quantity
            var:bfl_inv_10_offer = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_mode
            bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_10_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_10_offer = 1 bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes }
                    AND = { var:bfl_inv_10_offer = 2 bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes } }
            bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes
            NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_guard }
        }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = { value = var:bfl_inv_10_price add = 1 } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = var:bfl_inv_10_offer }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes }
                OR = { var:bfl_inv_10_offer = 1 bfl_free_cash >= { value = var:bfl_inv_10_price multiply = 1.25 } } }
                set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_10_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_10_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_state value = var:bfl_inv_10_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_accepted_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_10_quantity }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_quote multiply = -1 } }
                add_gold = bfl_goods_quote
                change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_10_state = 1 }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_10_price subtract = var:bfl_inv_10_basis } }
                    bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_basis value = 0 }
                } else = {
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_escrow value = var:bfl_inv_10_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_margin value = var:bfl_inv_10_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 3 } }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables add = var:bfl_inv_10_escrow }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = { value = var:bfl_inv_10_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_10_margin } }
                }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_offer value = 0 }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { remove_variable = bfl_goods_quote_bank }
                remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
                if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            }
        }
}
bfl_deliver_goods_10 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_10_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_10_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_10_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            bfl_goods_access_10 = yes var:bfl_inv_10_storage_due = 0
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { gold >= 1 exists = capital } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_10_market = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                bfl_redeem_goods_10 = yes
            } else = {
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = -1 }
                if = { limit = { exists = var:bfl_inv_10_host country_exists = var:bfl_inv_10_host } var:bfl_inv_10_host = { add_gold = 1 } }
                else = { add_gold = 1 change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_destination value = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_transit_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                # The accepted custody terms assign physical transport loss to the holder.
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_quantity multiply = 0.99 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_10_quantity }
            }
        }
}
bfl_redeem_goods_10 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_10_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_10_state = 1
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_10_holder exists = var:bfl_inv_10_holder.capital
            var:bfl_inv_10_storage_due = 0
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_10_transit_due = 0 var:bfl_inv_10_market = var:bfl_inv_10_holder.capital.market bfl_goods_access_10 = yes }
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_10_transit_due > 0 bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_10_transit_due var:bfl_inv_10_destination = var:bfl_inv_10_holder.capital.market } } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
        set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_before value = var:bfl_inv_10_holder.gold }
var:bfl_inv_10_holder = { sell_goods_to_market = { market = capital.market goods = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_10_type amount = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_10_quantity } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_10_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = var:bfl_inv_10_holder.gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_holder_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_10_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_10_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_state value = 3 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_encumbered value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_transit_due value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_10_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_10_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_transfer_goods_10 = {
save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = var:bfl_inv_10_price }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_10_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_10_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_10_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_seller country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_seller
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { scope:bfl_goods_party = scope:bfl_goods_seller }
            var:bfl_inv_10_transit_due = 0 bfl_goods_access_10 = yes
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes } }
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_10_price multiply = -1 } }
            scope:bfl_goods_seller = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_10_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                scope:bfl_goods_seller = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_10_price subtract = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_10_holder_basis } } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_10_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_settle_goods_forward_10 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_10_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_10_state = 2
            bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_10_due bfl_goods_access_10 = yes
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_10_holder gold >= { value = var:bfl_inv_10_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_10_margin } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_shortfall value = { value = 1 subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_10_quantity divide = var:bfl_inv_10_accepted_quantity } multiply = var:bfl_inv_10_price min = 0 max = var:bfl_inv_10_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_refund value = { value = var:bfl_goods_shortfall multiply = 2 } }
            add_gold = { value = bfl_goods_refund multiply = -1 }
            var:bfl_inv_10_holder = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.bfl_goods_refund }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_refund > var:bfl_inv_10_holder_basis var:bfl_inv_10_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_10_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_10_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_holder_basis subtract = bfl_goods_refund }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_holder_basis value = { value = var:bfl_inv_10_holder_basis min = 0 } }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables subtract = var:bfl_inv_10_escrow }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_10_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_10_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_10_price subtract = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_10_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_basis value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_escrow value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_margin value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_state value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_10_quantity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_state value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_encumbered value = 0 }
            }
            bfl_value_goods = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_10_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_10_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_enforce_goods_10 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_10_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_10_state < 4
            var:bfl_inv_10_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            NOT = { bfl_goods_branch_access = yes } var:bfl_inv_10_transit_due = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_state_before_seizure value = var:bfl_inv_10_state }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_state value = 4 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_offer value = 0 }
        }
}
bfl_release_goods_10 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_10_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_10_state = 4
            var:bfl_inv_10_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party bfl_goods_branch_access = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_state value = var:bfl_inv_10_state_before_seizure }
        }
}
bfl_margin_goods_10 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_10_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_10_state = 2
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_required_margin value = { value = bfl_goods_spot_10 min = var:bfl_inv_10_price } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_margin_call value = { value = var:bfl_goods_required_margin subtract = var:bfl_inv_10_margin } }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 bfl_free_cash >= bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_margin add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
            } else_if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_10_due value = bfl_period }
                bfl_settle_goods_forward_10 = yes
            }
        }
}
bfl_sell_goods_11 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_sellable_11 = yes }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cash_before value = gold }
        sell_goods_to_market = { market = var:bfl_inv_11_market goods = var:bfl_inv_11_type amount = var:bfl_inv_11_quantity }
set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_cash_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_11_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_basis value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_offer value = 0 }
            remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
        }
}
bfl_list_goods_1_11 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_11 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_offer value = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_11_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}

                bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }

                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }

bfl_quote_goods_11 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_11 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_11_offer > 0 bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_11_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            scope:bfl_goods_party = {
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_bank value = scope:bfl_goods_bank }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_slot value = 11 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_11_offer_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_11_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_price value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_11_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_quantity value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_11_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_mode value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_11_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_good value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_11_good }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_type value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_11_type }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_expiry value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_11_offer_expiry }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_quote_mode = 1 } trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.2 } else = { trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.6 }
            }
        }
}
bfl_accept_goods_11 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_11_offer > 0 bfl_goods_offerable_11 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_11_offer_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_generation
            var:bfl_inv_11_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation
            var:bfl_inv_11_price = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_price
            var:bfl_inv_11_quantity = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_quantity
            var:bfl_inv_11_offer = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_mode
            bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_11_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_11_offer = 1 bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes }
                    AND = { var:bfl_inv_11_offer = 2 bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes } }
            bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes
            NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_guard }
        }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = { value = var:bfl_inv_11_price add = 1 } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = var:bfl_inv_11_offer }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes }
                OR = { var:bfl_inv_11_offer = 1 bfl_free_cash >= { value = var:bfl_inv_11_price multiply = 1.25 } } }
                set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_11_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_11_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_state value = var:bfl_inv_11_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_accepted_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_11_quantity }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_quote multiply = -1 } }
                add_gold = bfl_goods_quote
                change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_11_state = 1 }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_11_price subtract = var:bfl_inv_11_basis } }
                    bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_basis value = 0 }
                } else = {
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_escrow value = var:bfl_inv_11_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_margin value = var:bfl_inv_11_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 3 } }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables add = var:bfl_inv_11_escrow }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = { value = var:bfl_inv_11_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_11_margin } }
                }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_offer value = 0 }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { remove_variable = bfl_goods_quote_bank }
                remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
                if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            }
        }
}

            scope:bfl_goods_seller = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_11_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                scope:bfl_goods_seller = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_11_price subtract = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_11_holder_basis } } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_11_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_settle_goods_forward_11 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_11_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_11_state = 2
            bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_11_due bfl_goods_access_11 = yes
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_11_holder gold >= { value = var:bfl_inv_11_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_11_margin } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_shortfall value = { value = 1 subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_11_quantity divide = var:bfl_inv_11_accepted_quantity } multiply = var:bfl_inv_11_price min = 0 max = var:bfl_inv_11_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_refund value = { value = var:bfl_goods_shortfall multiply = 2 } }
            add_gold = { value = bfl_goods_refund multiply = -1 }
            var:bfl_inv_11_holder = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.bfl_goods_refund }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_refund > var:bfl_inv_11_holder_basis var:bfl_inv_11_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_11_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_11_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_holder_basis subtract = bfl_goods_refund }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_holder_basis value = { value = var:bfl_inv_11_holder_basis min = 0 } }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables subtract = var:bfl_inv_11_escrow }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_11_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_11_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_11_price subtract = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_11_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_basis value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_escrow value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_margin value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_state value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_11_quantity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_state value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_encumbered value = 0 }
            }
            bfl_value_goods = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_11_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_11_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_enforce_goods_11 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_11_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_11_state < 4
            var:bfl_inv_11_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            NOT = { bfl_goods_branch_access = yes } var:bfl_inv_11_transit_due = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_state_before_seizure value = var:bfl_inv_11_state }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_state value = 4 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_offer value = 0 }
        }
}
bfl_release_goods_11 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_11_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_11_state = 4
            var:bfl_inv_11_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party bfl_goods_branch_access = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_state value = var:bfl_inv_11_state_before_seizure }
        }
}
bfl_margin_goods_11 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_11_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_11_state = 2
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_required_margin value = { value = bfl_goods_spot_11 min = var:bfl_inv_11_price } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_margin_call value = { value = var:bfl_goods_required_margin subtract = var:bfl_inv_11_margin } }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 bfl_free_cash >= bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_margin add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
            } else_if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_11_due value = bfl_period }
                bfl_settle_goods_forward_11 = yes
            }
        }
}
bfl_sell_goods_12 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_sellable_12 = yes }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cash_before value = gold }
        sell_goods_to_market = { market = var:bfl_inv_12_market goods = var:bfl_inv_12_type amount = var:bfl_inv_12_quantity }
set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_cash_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_12_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_basis value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_offer value = 0 }
            remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
        }
}
bfl_list_goods_1_12 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_12 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_offer value = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_12_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_list_goods_2_12 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_12 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_offer value = 2 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_12_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_quote_goods_12 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_12 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_12_offer > 0 bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_12_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            scope:bfl_goods_party = {
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_bank value = scope:bfl_goods_bank }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_slot value = 12 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_12_offer_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_12_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_price value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_12_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_quantity value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_12_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_mode value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_12_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_good value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_12_good }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_type value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_12_type }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_expiry value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_12_offer_expiry }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_quote_mode = 1 } trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.2 } else = { trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.6 }
            }
        }
}
bfl_accept_goods_12 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_12_offer > 0 bfl_goods_offerable_12 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_12_offer_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_generation
            var:bfl_inv_12_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation
            var:bfl_inv_12_price = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_price
            var:bfl_inv_12_quantity = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_quantity
            var:bfl_inv_12_offer = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_mode
            bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_12_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_12_offer = 1 bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes }
                    AND = { var:bfl_inv_12_offer = 2 bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes } }
            bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes
            NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_guard }
        }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = { value = var:bfl_inv_12_price add = 1 } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = var:bfl_inv_12_offer }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes }
                OR = { var:bfl_inv_12_offer = 1 bfl_free_cash >= { value = var:bfl_inv_12_price multiply = 1.25 } } }
                set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_12_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_12_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_state value = var:bfl_inv_12_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_accepted_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_12_quantity }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_quote multiply = -1 } }
                add_gold = bfl_goods_quote
                change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_12_state = 1 }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_12_price subtract = var:bfl_inv_12_basis } }
                    bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_basis value = 0 }
                } else = {
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_escrow value = var:bfl_inv_12_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_margin value = var:bfl_inv_12_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 3 } }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables add = var:bfl_inv_12_escrow }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = { value = var:bfl_inv_12_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_12_margin } }
                }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_offer value = 0 }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { remove_variable = bfl_goods_quote_bank }
                remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
                if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            }
        }
}
bfl_deliver_goods_12 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_12_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_12_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_12_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            bfl_goods_access_12 = yes var:bfl_inv_12_storage_due = 0
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { gold >= 1 exists = capital } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_12_market = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                bfl_redeem_goods_12 = yes
            } else = {
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = -1 }
                if = { limit = { exists = var:bfl_inv_12_host country_exists = var:bfl_inv_12_host } var:bfl_inv_12_host = { add_gold = 1 } }
                else = { add_gold = 1 change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_destination value = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_transit_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                # The accepted custody terms assign physical transport loss to the holder.
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_quantity multiply = 0.99 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_12_quantity }
            }
        }
}
bfl_redeem_goods_12 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_12_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_12_state = 1
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_12_holder exists = var:bfl_inv_12_holder.capital
            var:bfl_inv_12_storage_due = 0
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_12_transit_due = 0 var:bfl_inv_12_market = var:bfl_inv_12_holder.capital.market bfl_goods_access_12 = yes }
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_12_transit_due > 0 bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_12_transit_due var:bfl_inv_12_destination = var:bfl_inv_12_holder.capital.market } } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
        set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_before value = var:bfl_inv_12_holder.gold }
var:bfl_inv_12_holder = { sell_goods_to_market = { market = capital.market goods = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_12_type amount = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_12_quantity } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_12_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = var:bfl_inv_12_holder.gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_holder_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_12_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_12_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_state value = 3 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_encumbered value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_transit_due value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_12_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_12_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_transfer_goods_12 = {
save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = var:bfl_inv_12_price }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_12_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_12_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_12_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_seller country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_seller
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { scope:bfl_goods_party = scope:bfl_goods_seller }
            var:bfl_inv_12_transit_due = 0 bfl_goods_access_12 = yes
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes } }
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_12_price multiply = -1 } }
            scope:bfl_goods_seller = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_12_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                scope:bfl_goods_seller = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_12_price subtract = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_12_holder_basis } } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_12_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_12_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}

bfl_sell_goods_13 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_sellable_13 = yes }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cash_before value = gold }
        sell_goods_to_market = { market = var:bfl_inv_13_market goods = var:bfl_inv_13_type amount = var:bfl_inv_13_quantity }
set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_cash_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_13_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_basis value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_offer value = 0 }
            remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
        }
}
bfl_list_goods_1_13 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_13 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_offer value = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_13_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_list_goods_2_13 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_13 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_offer value = 2 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_13_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_quote_goods_13 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_13 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_13_offer > 0 bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_13_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            scope:bfl_goods_party = {
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_bank value = scope:bfl_goods_bank }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_slot value = 13 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_13_offer_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_13_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_price value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_13_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_quantity value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_13_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_mode value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_13_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_good value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_13_good }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_type value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_13_type }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_expiry value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_13_offer_expiry }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_quote_mode = 1 } trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.2 } else = { trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.6 }
            }
        }
}
bfl_accept_goods_13 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_13_offer > 0 bfl_goods_offerable_13 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_13_offer_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_generation
            var:bfl_inv_13_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation
            var:bfl_inv_13_price = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_price
            var:bfl_inv_13_quantity = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_quantity
            var:bfl_inv_13_offer = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_mode
            bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_13_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_13_offer = 1 bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes }
                    AND = { var:bfl_inv_13_offer = 2 bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes } }
            bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes
            NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_guard }
        }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = { value = var:bfl_inv_13_price add = 1 } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = var:bfl_inv_13_offer }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes }
                OR = { var:bfl_inv_13_offer = 1 bfl_free_cash >= { value = var:bfl_inv_13_price multiply = 1.25 } } }
                set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_13_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_13_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_state value = var:bfl_inv_13_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_accepted_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_13_quantity }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_quote multiply = -1 } }
                add_gold = bfl_goods_quote
                change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_13_state = 1 }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_13_price subtract = var:bfl_inv_13_basis } }
                    bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_basis value = 0 }
                } else = {
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_escrow value = var:bfl_inv_13_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_margin value = var:bfl_inv_13_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 3 } }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables add = var:bfl_inv_13_escrow }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = { value = var:bfl_inv_13_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_13_margin } }
                }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_offer value = 0 }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { remove_variable = bfl_goods_quote_bank }
                remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
                if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            }
        }
}
bfl_deliver_goods_13 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_13_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_13_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_13_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            bfl_goods_access_13 = yes var:bfl_inv_13_storage_due = 0
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { gold >= 1 exists = capital } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_13_market = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                bfl_redeem_goods_13 = yes
            } else = {
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = -1 }
                if = { limit = { exists = var:bfl_inv_13_host country_exists = var:bfl_inv_13_host } var:bfl_inv_13_host = { add_gold = 1 } }
                else = { add_gold = 1 change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_destination value = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_transit_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                # The accepted custody terms assign physical transport loss to the holder.
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_quantity multiply = 0.99 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_13_quantity }
            }
        }
}
bfl_redeem_goods_13 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_13_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_13_state = 1
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_13_holder exists = var:bfl_inv_13_holder.capital
            var:bfl_inv_13_storage_due = 0
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_13_transit_due = 0 var:bfl_inv_13_market = var:bfl_inv_13_holder.capital.market bfl_goods_access_13 = yes }
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_13_transit_due > 0 bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_13_transit_due var:bfl_inv_13_destination = var:bfl_inv_13_holder.capital.market } } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
        set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_before value = var:bfl_inv_13_holder.gold }
var:bfl_inv_13_holder = { sell_goods_to_market = { market = capital.market goods = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_13_type amount = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_13_quantity } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_13_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = var:bfl_inv_13_holder.gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_holder_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_13_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_13_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_state value = 3 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_encumbered value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_transit_due value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_13_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_13_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_transfer_goods_13 = {
save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = var:bfl_inv_13_price }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_13_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_13_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_13_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_seller country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_seller
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { scope:bfl_goods_party = scope:bfl_goods_seller }
            var:bfl_inv_13_transit_due = 0 bfl_goods_access_13 = yes
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes } }
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_13_price multiply = -1 } }
            scope:bfl_goods_seller = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_13_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                scope:bfl_goods_seller = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_13_price subtract = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_13_holder_basis } } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_13_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_settle_goods_forward_13 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_13_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_13_state = 2
            bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_13_due bfl_goods_access_13 = yes
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_13_holder gold >= { value = var:bfl_inv_13_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_13_margin } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_shortfall value = { value = 1 subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_13_quantity divide = var:bfl_inv_13_accepted_quantity } multiply = var:bfl_inv_13_price min = 0 max = var:bfl_inv_13_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_refund value = { value = var:bfl_goods_shortfall multiply = 2 } }
            add_gold = { value = bfl_goods_refund multiply = -1 }
            var:bfl_inv_13_holder = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.bfl_goods_refund }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_refund > var:bfl_inv_13_holder_basis var:bfl_inv_13_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_13_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_13_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_holder_basis subtract = bfl_goods_refund }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_holder_basis value = { value = var:bfl_inv_13_holder_basis min = 0 } }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables subtract = var:bfl_inv_13_escrow }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_13_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_13_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_13_price subtract = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_13_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_basis value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_escrow value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_margin value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_state value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_13_quantity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_state value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_encumbered value = 0 }
            }
            bfl_value_goods = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_13_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_13_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_enforce_goods_13 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_13_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_13_state < 4
            var:bfl_inv_13_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            NOT = { bfl_goods_branch_access = yes } var:bfl_inv_13_transit_due = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_state_before_seizure value = var:bfl_inv_13_state }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_state value = 4 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_offer value = 0 }
        }
}
bfl_release_goods_13 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_13_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_13_state = 4
            var:bfl_inv_13_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party bfl_goods_branch_access = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_state value = var:bfl_inv_13_state_before_seizure }
        }
}
bfl_margin_goods_13 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_13_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_13_state = 2
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_required_margin value = { value = bfl_goods_spot_13 min = var:bfl_inv_13_price } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_margin_call value = { value = var:bfl_goods_required_margin subtract = var:bfl_inv_13_margin } }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 bfl_free_cash >= bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_margin add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
            } else_if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_13_due value = bfl_period }
                bfl_settle_goods_forward_13 = yes
            }
        }
}
bfl_sell_goods_14 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_sellable_14 = yes }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cash_before value = gold }
        sell_goods_to_market = { market = var:bfl_inv_14_market goods = var:bfl_inv_14_type amount = var:bfl_inv_14_quantity }
set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_cash_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_14_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_basis value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_offer value = 0 }
            remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
        }
}
