            }
        }
}
bfl_accept_goods_14 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_14_offer > 0 bfl_goods_offerable_14 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_14_offer_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_generation
            var:bfl_inv_14_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation
            var:bfl_inv_14_price = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_price
            var:bfl_inv_14_quantity = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_quantity
            var:bfl_inv_14_offer = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_mode
            bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_14_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_14_offer = 1 bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes }
                    AND = { var:bfl_inv_14_offer = 2 bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes } }
            bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes
            NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_guard }
        }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = { value = var:bfl_inv_14_price add = 1 } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = var:bfl_inv_14_offer }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes }
                OR = { var:bfl_inv_14_offer = 1 bfl_free_cash >= { value = var:bfl_inv_14_price multiply = 1.25 } } }
                set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_14_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_14_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_state value = var:bfl_inv_14_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_accepted_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_14_quantity }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_quote multiply = -1 } }
                add_gold = bfl_goods_quote
                change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_14_state = 1 }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_14_price subtract = var:bfl_inv_14_basis } }
                    bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_basis value = 0 }
                } else = {
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_escrow value = var:bfl_inv_14_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_margin value = var:bfl_inv_14_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 3 } }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables add = var:bfl_inv_14_escrow }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = { value = var:bfl_inv_14_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_14_margin } }
                }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_offer value = 0 }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { remove_variable = bfl_goods_quote_bank }
                remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
                if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            }
        }
}
bfl_deliver_goods_14 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_14_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_14_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_14_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            bfl_goods_access_14 = yes var:bfl_inv_14_storage_due = 0
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { gold >= 1 exists = capital } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_14_market = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                bfl_redeem_goods_14 = yes
            } else = {
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = -1 }
                if = { limit = { exists = var:bfl_inv_14_host country_exists = var:bfl_inv_14_host } var:bfl_inv_14_host = { add_gold = 1 } }
                else = { add_gold = 1 change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_destination value = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_transit_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                # The accepted custody terms assign physical transport loss to the holder.
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_quantity multiply = 0.99 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_14_quantity }
            }
        }
}
bfl_redeem_goods_14 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_14_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_14_state = 1
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_14_holder exists = var:bfl_inv_14_holder.capital
            var:bfl_inv_14_storage_due = 0
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_14_transit_due = 0 var:bfl_inv_14_market = var:bfl_inv_14_holder.capital.market bfl_goods_access_14 = yes }
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_14_transit_due > 0 bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_14_transit_due var:bfl_inv_14_destination = var:bfl_inv_14_holder.capital.market } } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
        set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_before value = var:bfl_inv_14_holder.gold }
var:bfl_inv_14_holder = { sell_goods_to_market = { market = capital.market goods = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_14_type amount = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_14_quantity } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_14_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = var:bfl_inv_14_holder.gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_holder_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_14_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_14_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_state value = 3 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_encumbered value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_transit_due value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_14_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_14_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_transfer_goods_14 = {
save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = var:bfl_inv_14_price }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_14_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_14_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_14_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_seller country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_seller
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { scope:bfl_goods_party = scope:bfl_goods_seller }
            var:bfl_inv_14_transit_due = 0 bfl_goods_access_14 = yes
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes } }
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_14_price multiply = -1 } }
            scope:bfl_goods_seller = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_14_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                scope:bfl_goods_seller = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_14_price subtract = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_14_holder_basis } } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_14_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_settle_goods_forward_14 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_14_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_14_state = 2
            bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_14_due bfl_goods_access_14 = yes
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_14_holder gold >= { value = var:bfl_inv_14_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_14_margin } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_shortfall value = { value = 1 subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_14_quantity divide = var:bfl_inv_14_accepted_quantity } multiply = var:bfl_inv_14_price min = 0 max = var:bfl_inv_14_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_refund value = { value = var:bfl_goods_shortfall multiply = 2 } }
            add_gold = { value = bfl_goods_refund multiply = -1 }
            var:bfl_inv_14_holder = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.bfl_goods_refund }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_refund > var:bfl_inv_14_holder_basis var:bfl_inv_14_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_14_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_14_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_holder_basis subtract = bfl_goods_refund }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_holder_basis value = { value = var:bfl_inv_14_holder_basis min = 0 } }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables subtract = var:bfl_inv_14_escrow }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_14_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_14_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_14_price subtract = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_14_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_basis value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_escrow value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_margin value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_state value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_14_quantity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_state value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_encumbered value = 0 }
            }
            bfl_value_goods = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_14_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_14_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_enforce_goods_14 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_14_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_14_state < 4
            var:bfl_inv_14_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            NOT = { bfl_goods_branch_access = yes } var:bfl_inv_14_transit_due = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_state_before_seizure value = var:bfl_inv_14_state }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_state value = 4 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_offer value = 0 }
        }
}
bfl_release_goods_14 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_14_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_14_state = 4
            var:bfl_inv_14_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party bfl_goods_branch_access = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_state value = var:bfl_inv_14_state_before_seizure }
        }
}
bfl_margin_goods_14 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_14_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_14_state = 2
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_required_margin value = { value = bfl_goods_spot_14 min = var:bfl_inv_14_price } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_margin_call value = { value = var:bfl_goods_required_margin subtract = var:bfl_inv_14_margin } }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 bfl_free_cash >= bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_margin add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
            } else_if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_14_due value = bfl_period }
                bfl_settle_goods_forward_14 = yes
            }
        }
}
bfl_sell_goods_15 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_sellable_15 = yes }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cash_before value = gold }
        sell_goods_to_market = { market = var:bfl_inv_15_market goods = var:bfl_inv_15_type amount = var:bfl_inv_15_quantity }
set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_cash_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_15_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_basis value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_offer value = 0 }
            remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
        }
}
bfl_list_goods_1_15 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_15 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_offer value = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_15_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_list_goods_2_15 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_15 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_offer value = 2 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_15_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_quote_goods_15 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_15 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_15_offer > 0 bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_15_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            scope:bfl_goods_party = {
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_bank value = scope:bfl_goods_bank }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_slot value = 15 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_15_offer_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_15_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_price value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_15_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_quantity value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_15_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_mode value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_15_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_good value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_15_good }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_type value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_15_type }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_expiry value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_15_offer_expiry }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_quote_mode = 1 } trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.2 } else = { trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.6 }
            }
        }
}
bfl_accept_goods_15 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_15_offer > 0 bfl_goods_offerable_15 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_15_offer_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_generation
            var:bfl_inv_15_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation
            var:bfl_inv_15_price = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_price
            var:bfl_inv_15_quantity = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_quantity
            var:bfl_inv_15_offer = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_mode
            bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_15_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_15_offer = 1 bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes }
                    AND = { var:bfl_inv_15_offer = 2 bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes } }
            bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes
            NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_guard }
        }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = { value = var:bfl_inv_15_price add = 1 } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = var:bfl_inv_15_offer }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes }
                OR = { var:bfl_inv_15_offer = 1 bfl_free_cash >= { value = var:bfl_inv_15_price multiply = 1.25 } } }
                set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_15_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_15_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_state value = var:bfl_inv_15_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_accepted_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_15_quantity }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_quote multiply = -1 } }
                add_gold = bfl_goods_quote
                change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_15_state = 1 }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_15_price subtract = var:bfl_inv_15_basis } }
                    bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_basis value = 0 }
                } else = {
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_escrow value = var:bfl_inv_15_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_margin value = var:bfl_inv_15_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 3 } }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables add = var:bfl_inv_15_escrow }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = { value = var:bfl_inv_15_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_15_margin } }
                }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_offer value = 0 }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { remove_variable = bfl_goods_quote_bank }
                remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
                if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            }
        }
}

bfl_settle_goods_forward_15 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_15_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_15_state = 2
            bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_15_due bfl_goods_access_15 = yes
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_15_holder gold >= { value = var:bfl_inv_15_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_15_margin } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_shortfall value = { value = 1 subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_15_quantity divide = var:bfl_inv_15_accepted_quantity } multiply = var:bfl_inv_15_price min = 0 max = var:bfl_inv_15_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_refund value = { value = var:bfl_goods_shortfall multiply = 2 } }
            add_gold = { value = bfl_goods_refund multiply = -1 }
            var:bfl_inv_15_holder = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.bfl_goods_refund }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_refund > var:bfl_inv_15_holder_basis var:bfl_inv_15_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_15_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_15_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_holder_basis subtract = bfl_goods_refund }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_holder_basis value = { value = var:bfl_inv_15_holder_basis min = 0 } }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables subtract = var:bfl_inv_15_escrow }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_15_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_15_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_15_price subtract = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_15_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_basis value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_escrow value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_margin value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_state value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_15_quantity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_state value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_encumbered value = 0 }
            }
            bfl_value_goods = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_15_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_15_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_enforce_goods_15 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_15_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_15_state < 4
            var:bfl_inv_15_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            NOT = { bfl_goods_branch_access = yes } var:bfl_inv_15_transit_due = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_state_before_seizure value = var:bfl_inv_15_state }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_state value = 4 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_offer value = 0 }
        }
}
bfl_release_goods_15 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_15_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_15_state = 4
            var:bfl_inv_15_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party bfl_goods_branch_access = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_state value = var:bfl_inv_15_state_before_seizure }
        }
}
bfl_margin_goods_15 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_15_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_15_state = 2
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_required_margin value = { value = bfl_goods_spot_15 min = var:bfl_inv_15_price } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_margin_call value = { value = var:bfl_goods_required_margin subtract = var:bfl_inv_15_margin } }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 bfl_free_cash >= bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_margin add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
            } else_if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_15_due value = bfl_period }
                bfl_settle_goods_forward_15 = yes
            }
        }
}
bfl_sell_goods_16 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_sellable_16 = yes }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_cash_before value = gold }
        sell_goods_to_market = { market = var:bfl_inv_16_market goods = var:bfl_inv_16_type amount = var:bfl_inv_16_quantity }
set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_cash_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_16_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_basis value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_offer value = 0 }
            remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
        }
}
bfl_list_goods_1_16 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_16 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_offer value = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_16_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_list_goods_2_16 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_16 = yes
                bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_offer value = 2 }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_offer_generation add = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_offer_expiry value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_price value = { value = var:bfl_inv_16_basis multiply = 1.05 multiply = 100 add = 0.99999 floor = yes divide = 100 } }
            }
}
bfl_quote_goods_16 = {
if = { limit = { bfl_goods_offerable_16 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_16_offer > 0 bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_16_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            scope:bfl_goods_party = {
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_bank value = scope:bfl_goods_bank }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_slot value = 16 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_offer_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_generation }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_price value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_quantity value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_mode value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_good value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_good }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_type value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_type }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_expiry value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_offer_expiry }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_goods_quote_mode = 1 } trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.2 } else = { trigger_event_non_silently = bfl_goods.6 }
            }
        }
}
bfl_accept_goods_16 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_offer > 0 bfl_goods_offerable_16 = yes
            var:bfl_inv_16_offer_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_generation
            var:bfl_inv_16_generation = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_lot_generation
            var:bfl_inv_16_price = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_price
            var:bfl_inv_16_quantity = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_quantity
            var:bfl_inv_16_offer = scope:bfl_goods_party.var:bfl_goods_quote_mode
            bfl_period <= var:bfl_inv_16_offer_expiry
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { this = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_16_offer = 1 bfl_can_originate_warehouse_receipt = yes }
                    AND = { var:bfl_inv_16_offer = 2 bfl_can_originate_commodity_forward = yes } }
            bfl_goods_researched_collateral = yes
            NOT = { has_variable = bfl_goods_guard }
        }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = { value = var:bfl_inv_16_price add = 1 } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = var:bfl_inv_16_offer }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes }
                OR = { var:bfl_inv_16_offer = 1 bfl_free_cash >= { value = var:bfl_inv_16_price multiply = 1.25 } } }
                set_variable = bfl_goods_guard
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_16_price }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_16_quantity }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_state value = var:bfl_inv_16_offer }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_accepted_quantity value = var:bfl_inv_16_quantity }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_quote multiply = -1 } }
                add_gold = bfl_goods_quote
                change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 }
                if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_state = 1 }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_16_price subtract = var:bfl_inv_16_basis } }
                    bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_basis value = 0 }
                } else = {
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_escrow value = var:bfl_inv_16_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_margin value = var:bfl_inv_16_price }
                    set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 3 } }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables add = var:bfl_inv_16_escrow }
                    change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = { value = var:bfl_inv_16_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_16_margin } }
                }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_offer value = 0 }
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { remove_variable = bfl_goods_quote_bank }
                remove_variable = bfl_goods_guard bfl_value_goods = yes
                if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            }
        }
}
bfl_deliver_goods_16 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_16_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_16_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            bfl_goods_access_16 = yes var:bfl_inv_16_storage_due = 0
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { gold >= 1 exists = capital } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_market = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                bfl_redeem_goods_16 = yes
            } else = {
                scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = -1 }
                if = { limit = { exists = var:bfl_inv_16_host country_exists = var:bfl_inv_16_host } var:bfl_inv_16_host = { add_gold = 1 } }
                else = { add_gold = 1 change_variable = { name = bfl_profit_services add = 1 } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_destination value = scope:bfl_goods_party.capital.market }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_transit_due value = { value = bfl_period add = 2 } }
                # The accepted custody terms assign physical transport loss to the holder.
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_quantity multiply = 0.99 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_encumbered value = var:bfl_inv_16_quantity }
            }
        }
}
bfl_redeem_goods_16 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_16_state = 1
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_16_holder exists = var:bfl_inv_16_holder.capital
            var:bfl_inv_16_storage_due = 0
            OR = { AND = { var:bfl_inv_16_transit_due = 0 var:bfl_inv_16_market = var:bfl_inv_16_holder.capital.market bfl_goods_access_16 = yes }
                AND = { var:bfl_inv_16_transit_due > 0 bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_16_transit_due var:bfl_inv_16_destination = var:bfl_inv_16_holder.capital.market } } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
        set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_before value = var:bfl_inv_16_holder.gold }
var:bfl_inv_16_holder = { sell_goods_to_market = { market = capital.market goods = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_type amount = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_quantity } }
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = var:bfl_inv_16_holder.gold subtract = var:bfl_goods_holder_before subtract = var:bfl_inv_16_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_16_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_state value = 3 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_quantity value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_encumbered value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_transit_due value = 0 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_16_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_transfer_goods_16 = {
save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote value = var:bfl_inv_16_price }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_quote_kind value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_16_state = 1
            var:bfl_inv_16_holder ?= scope:bfl_goods_seller country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_seller
            country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party NOT = { scope:bfl_goods_party = scope:bfl_goods_seller }
            var:bfl_inv_16_transit_due = 0 bfl_goods_access_16 = yes
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_goods_party_funded = yes } }
            scope:bfl_goods_party = { add_gold = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_price multiply = -1 } }
            scope:bfl_goods_seller = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                scope:bfl_goods_seller = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_price subtract = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_inv_16_holder_basis } } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_holder value = scope:bfl_goods_party }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_holder_basis value = var:bfl_inv_16_price }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_seller = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
            if = { limit = { scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } scope:bfl_goods_party = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_settle_goods_forward_16 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_16_state = 2
            bfl_period >= var:bfl_inv_16_due bfl_goods_access_16 = yes
            country_exists = var:bfl_inv_16_holder gold >= { value = var:bfl_inv_16_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_16_margin } }
            save_scope_as = bfl_goods_bank
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_shortfall value = { value = 1 subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_16_quantity divide = var:bfl_inv_16_accepted_quantity } multiply = var:bfl_inv_16_price min = 0 max = var:bfl_inv_16_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_refund value = { value = var:bfl_goods_shortfall multiply = 2 } }
            add_gold = { value = bfl_goods_refund multiply = -1 }
            var:bfl_inv_16_holder = { add_gold = scope:bfl_goods_bank.bfl_goods_refund }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_refund > var:bfl_inv_16_holder_basis var:bfl_inv_16_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_holder_gain value = { value = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_16_holder_basis } }
                var:bfl_inv_16_holder = { set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = scope:bfl_goods_bank.var:bfl_goods_holder_gain } bfl_book_goods_gain = yes }
            }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_holder_basis subtract = bfl_goods_refund }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_holder_basis value = { value = var:bfl_inv_16_holder_basis min = 0 } }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_other_payables subtract = var:bfl_inv_16_escrow }
            change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed subtract = { value = var:bfl_inv_16_escrow add = var:bfl_inv_16_margin } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_realized value = { value = var:bfl_inv_16_price subtract = bfl_goods_refund subtract = var:bfl_inv_16_basis } }
            bfl_book_goods_gain = yes
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_basis value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_escrow value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_margin value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_state value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_quantity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_active value = 0 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_state value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_encumbered value = 0 }
            }
            bfl_value_goods = yes
            if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_holder = { bfl_has_accounts = yes } } var:bfl_inv_16_holder = { bfl_value_goods = yes } }
        }
}
bfl_enforce_goods_16 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_16_state < 4
            var:bfl_inv_16_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party country_exists = scope:bfl_goods_party
            NOT = { bfl_goods_branch_access = yes } var:bfl_inv_16_transit_due = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_state_before_seizure value = var:bfl_inv_16_state }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_state value = 4 } set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_offer value = 0 }
        }
}
bfl_release_goods_16 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_16_state = 4
            var:bfl_inv_16_host ?= scope:bfl_goods_party bfl_goods_branch_access = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_state value = var:bfl_inv_16_state_before_seizure }
        }
}
bfl_margin_goods_16 = {
if = { limit = { var:bfl_inv_16_active = 1 var:bfl_inv_16_state = 2
            bfl_can_originate_margining = yes }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_required_margin value = { value = bfl_goods_spot_16 min = var:bfl_inv_16_price } }
            set_variable = { name = bfl_goods_margin_call value = { value = var:bfl_goods_required_margin subtract = var:bfl_inv_16_margin } }
            if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 bfl_free_cash >= bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_margin add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
                change_variable = { name = bfl_cash_committed add = bfl_goods_margin_call }
            } else_if = { limit = { bfl_goods_margin_call > 0 }
                set_variable = { name = bfl_inv_16_due value = bfl_period }
                bfl_settle_goods_forward_16 = yes
            }
        }
}
